Document 011: 2008年再现？CDS重回市场焦点

Source PDF: all report pdf/2008年再现？CDS重回市场焦点.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

AI 债务泡沫隐现，华尔街正以史上最快速度重演信⽤衍⽣品狂潮。 9 ⽉ AI 相关 CDS 周均交易
量突破 90 亿美元，较 7 ⽉暴涨 4.5 倍； CoreWeave 违约概率已飙⾄ 63% ，甲⻣⽂ CDS 年内
翻逾两倍。当真实资⾦账⼾沉寂 15 年后再度涌⼊单⼀名称 CDS 市场，上⼀次出现这⼀幕，
是 2007 年。
摘要：
AI 债务泡沫升温，信⽤违约互换交易量爆炸式增⻓，华尔街正在重演⼀场似曾相识的对冲狂潮。
据摩根⼠丹利最新研究数据，今年 9 ⽉，与甲⻣⽂、 SpaceX 、 Alphabet 、亚⻢逊、 Meta 、博通
及英伟达相关的信⽤违约互换（ CDS ）周均交易量突破 90 亿美元，较 7 ⽉的不⾜ 20 亿美元飙升逾
4.5 倍。
与此同时，⾼盛固定收益、货币及⼤宗商品（ FICC ）部⻔数据显⽰，AI 单⼀名称 CDS 交易量同
⽐增⻓ 400% ，流动性名称数量从 4 个扩展⾄ 10 ⾄ 12 个。这⼀规模扩张速度，令部分市场参与者
联想到 2007 ⾄ 2008 年⾦融危机前⼣的信⽤衍⽣品市场。

⾼盛信贷策略师 Avishek Biswas 在其 10 ⽉初的信贷市场策略报告中指出， AI 相关 CDS 的每名称
每周名义交易量，已从去年的 5000 万⾄ 1 亿美元跃升⾄如今的每⽇数亿美元量级。法国兴业银⾏
美国股票策略主管 Manish Kabra 表⽰， CDS 已成为衡量超⼤规模云计算企业（ Hyperscaler ）融
资状况及整体 AI 交易情绪的 " 关键⻛向标 " ，并将其列为该⾏ 2026 年四季度美国股票展望的核⼼交
易之⼀，直⾔投资者应 " 看 CDS ，⽽⾮看每股收益 " 。
AI 债务规模急剧膨胀， CDS 市场随之爆发
AI 热潮背后，是⼀场规模空前的债务扩张。据摩根⼠丹利数据，2026 年迄今， AI 相关企业已累
计发⾏约 4660 亿美元债券，较 2025 年同期的 1010 亿美元增⻓逾 3.6 倍。随着 AI 企业跃升为债券
市场最⼤借款⽅群体，围绕这些名称的违约保护需求也随之呈⼏何级数增⻓。
价格层⾯，市场已发出明显的压⼒信号。5 年期 Meta CDS 本⽉升⾄历史⾼点 108 个基点，英伟达

达到 101 个基点， Alphabet 与亚⻢逊的保护成本同样创下历史新⾼。甲⻣⽂ CDS 报价从年初的
144 个基点飙升⾄ 322 个基点， SpaceX 则从 6 ⽉底的 110 个基点翻倍⾄ 226 个基点的历史⾼位。
⾼盛跨资产信贷专家 Alisha Pasi 在其最新周报中披露，该⾏近期收到创纪录数量的⾮传统账⼾
对 CoreWeave 债务及甲⻣⽂ CDS 的询价，需求之旺盛促使⾼盛专⻔设⽴了⼀个 AI 跨资本结构投
资者电话会议，参与者涵盖科技、媒体及电信股票、可转债、投资级现⾦及 " 困境 /AI 债务交易 " 等
多个部⻔ —— 后者已成为⼀个独⽴的职能条线。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

为何选择 CDS ⽽⾮股票看跌期权
在对冲⼯具的选择上，市场正在发⽣结构性转变。法兴银⾏的 Kabra 直⾔，⽤股票看跌期权对冲
超⼤规模云计算企业的⻛险 " 极为困难，且成本极⾼ " 。这⼀判断解释了为何越来越多的股票账⼾
开始跨越资产类别，转向信⽤衍⽣品市场寻求保护。
摩根⼠丹利在其 10 ⽉ 1 ⽇发布的《 AI 融资现状：暂停⽽⾮撤退》报告中同样指出，买⼊ AI 相关
CDS 保护 " 仍是我们在信⽤衍⽣品市场参与 AI 主题的⾸选⽅式 " ，并预计随着银⾏需要管理对⼿⽅
敞⼝， " ⾮经济性对冲需求 " 将持续上升。
⾼盛 IG 交易员 Miles McCarthy 此前亦指出， CDS 买家来源多元：部分账⼾以杠杆或⽆资⾦信⽤
观点进⾏⽅向性交易，部分账⼾利⽤ CDS 与债券之间的相对价值错位套利，还有股票账⼾借此
对冲公开市场及私募 AI 相关股权组合。值得注意的是，随着英伟达、 AMD 、博通等芯⽚制造商
开始为买家提供卖⽅融资和担保，市场开始将其视同借款⽅，相关 CDS 交易量随之暴增约 10

倍。
此外，⾼盛信贷销售策略数据显⽰，上周 CDX IG 指数仅⾛阔 4.4 个基点⾄ 62.5 个基点，⽽核⼼ AI
名称单⽇便可移动 30 个基点。这⼀分化正是市场从指数对冲转向单⼀名称 CDS 的根本原因 ——
指数已⽆法精准捕捉投资者实际持有的⻛险敞⼝。
CoreWeave ：最脆弱的⼀环
在所有 AI 相关名称中， CoreWeave 是市场最为集中关注的压⼒点。据英国《⾦融时报》报道，
CoreWeave 的 5 年期 CDS ⽬前报价约 1090 个基点，购买 1000 万美元保护需预付 182 万美元，此
后每季度再⽀付 12.5 万美元 ——仅第⼀年的成本便约占名义本⾦的 23% 。
S&P Global 信⽤分析师 Gavan Nolan 指出， " ⼀旦某个名称的 CDS 交易价格超过 1000 个基点，定
价逻辑便开始转向预期回收率 " ，意味着市场已在为违约情景定价，⽽⾮仅仅对冲⻛险溢价。
⾼盛此前曾上调 CoreWeave 评级，但汇丰银⾏随即给出 39 美元⽬标价并发出警告，称 " 投⼊越

多，价值毁损越⼤ " 。 10 ⽉ 14 ⽇， CoreWeave CDS 盘中从 1000 个基点突破⾄ 1050 个基点，收盘
时进⼀步升⾄ 1117 个基点，隐含五年内违约概率约为 63% 。与此同时，韩国最⼤规模 IPO 、数据
中⼼运营商 Elice 在上市后数秒内即暴跌 32% ，进⼀步印证了市场对 AI 基础设施投资回报的疑
虑。
Polus Capital 的 Robert Dafforn 对英国《⾦融时报》表⽰， AI 融资链条中的循环性意味着 " ⽆论哪
个环节最为薄弱，都将率先拖累整个体系 " 。
从对冲⼯具到系统性⻛险信号
当前的 CDS 热潮是否预⽰着更深层的系统性⻛险？
从结构上看，需求具有⾃我强化的特征。⾼盛预测， 2027 年超⼤规模云计算企业直接发债规模
将再达 4200 亿美元，加上约 3000 亿美元的项⽬融资，每⼀笔新债的发⾏都将催⽣新的对冲需
求，每⼀个新设⽴的特殊⽬的载体（ SPV ）、租赁担保或供应商融资安排，都将产⽣新的对⼿⽅
⻛险敞⼝。
Pasi 在其报告中还指出，本周⾦融和保险板块的 CDS 同样受到追捧， " 市场担忧这些机构在 AI 交

易回调中的投资集中度⻛险 "—— 这意味着传导链条已从 AI 借款⽅延伸⾄持有其债券的⾦融机构
资产负债表。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

期市盈率估值则随之同步压缩，两者之间的背离正在收窄。

個股報告 
南亞科(2408) 
南以抗拒 亞倒群雄 科不容緩 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $800 
前次投資建議：2026.07.13： 
「買進」 ，目標價: $710

報告日期：2026.10.13 研 究 員：王穎

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 看好記憶體需求緊俏有利報價走升，維持「買進」 投資評等，目標價 800 元(6 x 27(F)PER)，
潛在上漲空間 51.8%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/10/12 收盤價： 527.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 1336.5 億元新台幣， QoQ+61.9%，YoY+ 
611.7%，毛利率 85.7% ，營業費用 75.88 億元，營業利
益為 1068.9億元 ， 業外收入24.38億 ， 稅後淨利為 876.3
億元﹐EPS 為 25.4 元，每股淨值為 118.99元。營收成
長及毛利雙雙成長受惠 平均銷售單價季增超過 六十位數
百分比，而季減中個位數百分比，匯率有利低個位數百分
比。 
➢ 展望 26Q4，受惠 AI 需求續強預期 DRAM 將持續缺貨，
且南亞科 AI相關產品已佔營收逾 20%，雖受限產能滿載
出貨量將無法再提升，然而預期合約價將持續於 26Q4上
漲 15~20%，營收及毛利率將持續創高。因此兆豐預估營
收 1556.04 億元，QoQ+16.4%，YoY+417.1%，毛利率 
88.1%，EPS為 30.35 元。 
➢ 展望 2026~2027 年，受惠 AI 需求持續強勁三大原廠於
DRAM產能持續滿載，預期 DDR5、DDR4、DDR3等產
品將持續供不應求。1C 產品預期於 27Q1 開始驗證，有
利打入 AI伺服器供應鏈，預期 AI 占比將逐季提高。因此
兆豐預估營收 4208.9/6993.1 億元，YoY+532.1/66.2%，
毛利率 83.3/86.3%，EPS 為 77.85/133.2 元。南亞科目
前位於(3.9 X 27(F) PER)，看好記憶體需求緊俏報價持續
走升， 維持「買進」投資評等，目標價  800 元(6 x 
27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 51.8%。

營 運 分 析

南亞科技為全球前四大 DRAM廠商 
南亞科技主要致力 DRAM (動態隨機存取記憶體)的研發、設計、製造與銷售，
開發自主技術，發展智慧財產權，培育關鍵技術研發團隊，落實綠色科技及
智慧製造全面提升製程效能與產能，是全球第4大的 DRAM公司。公司已成
功開發 10 奈米級 DRAM 製程技術，將可微縮至少 3 個世代，並用以發展
DDR5及 LPDDR5 等下世代產品，強化公司自主技術開發與製程轉換佈局。
第二世代 10 奈米級製程技術(1B)產品 16Gb DDR5 已完成設計與試產，於
2024 年量產。南亞科技具備完整 DRAM 產品線，可因應智慧世代的發展，
滿足多元化的產品需求。預期 1C產品於 27Q1 開始驗證。

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q3 ASP成長帶動營收毛利雙雙上升 
26Q3 營收  1336.5 億元新台幣， QoQ+61.9%，YoY+ 611.7%，毛利率
85.7% ，營業費用 75.88 億元，營業利益為 1068.9 億元，業外收入 24.38
億，稅後淨利為 876.3 億元﹐EPS 為 25.4 元，每股淨值為 118.99元。營
收成長及毛利雙雙成長受惠平均銷售單價季增超過六十位數百分比，而季減
中個位數百分比，匯率有利低個位數百分比。

26Q4合約價持續上漲  
展望 26Q4，受惠 AI 需求續強預期 DRAM 將持續缺貨，且南亞科 AI 相關產
品已佔營收逾 20%，雖受限產能滿載出貨量將無法再提升，然而預期合約價
將持續於 26Q4 上漲 15~20%，營收及毛利率將持續創高。因此兆豐預估營
收 1556.04 億元，QoQ+16.4%，YoY+417.1%，毛利率 88.1%，EPS 為 30.35
元。

續升高。

- 4 - 
個股報告

圖三、DRAM合約價報價走勢圖

資料來源：兆豐投顧整理

南亞科完成 787.2 億元私募案，攜手四大客戶搶佔 AI 供應鏈先機 
南亞科日前完成總額新台幣  787.2 億元的私募認購，吸引四大核心客戶參
與。增資後，相關投資方持股比例達 10.19%。雙方同時簽署供應協議，確
保未來能穩定供應多元 DRAM 產品組合。主要目的在於強化南亞科與客戶
的戰略合作，透過 e-SSD 與網通產品等領域，建立 AI 及雲端服務供應商
（CSP）供應鏈中的雙贏局面。私募資金將注資新廠以提升產能，進一步確
立南亞科在 AI 價值鏈中的市場地位。本次私募後，南亞科總發行股數將達 
34.5 億股。

記憶體供不應求，產能持續擴充 
目前產能為 6.5 萬片/月，產能利用率維持滿載，2026Q1~Q3 資本支出 276
億，2026 以不超過 697 億元為上限，設備佔比五成．5A新廠第一階段 35,900
片/月 於 2026~2029 資本支出約 3,466 億．新廠預計於 2027 年初開始裝機，
主要生產 1B/1C/1D製程，預期 27年底開出 20k/月，2028 年達每月投片量
達 30k/月，主要因應網通、eSSD等產品之強勁需求。

AI 帶動記憶體強勁需求，有利報價持續走升 
展望 2026~2027 年，受惠 AI 需求持續強勁三大原廠於 DRAM 產能持續滿
載，預期DDR5、DDR4、DDR3等產品將持續供不應求。1C產品預期於 27Q1
開始驗證，有利打入 AI 伺服器供應鏈，預期 AI 占比將逐季提高。因此兆豐
預估營收 4208.9/6993.1 億元，YoY+532.1/66.2%，毛利率83.3/86.3%，EPS
為 77.85/133.2 元。

記憶體持續供不應
求，報價持續走升。 
2027 新產能主要 用
於生產 1B/1C/1D 製
程。

穩定供應多元 DRAM
產品組合。

### Page 1

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
Recharging Growth: UPS Recovery and HVDC Potential; Initiate at Buy
CITI'S TAKE 
We initiate coverage on Voltronic with a Buy rating and NT$1,050 target 
price. Voltronic is a Taiwan-based UPS ODM, supplying to western power 
vendors including Schneider, Riello, Vertiv and Eaton. We view 2026E as the 
earnings trough, with improving online UPS mix and operating leverage 
driving 28% earnings rebound in 2027E. Structural outsourcing by western 
power vendors should support sustained share gains, while expansion into 
HVDC power racks offers a new stream for AIDC growth, supporting a 
potential re-rating. Our TP applies 25x 2027E EPS against earnings CAGR 
of 20% in 2026-2028E, broadly consistent with its L-T historical average.
Outsourcing and mix upgrade underpin the recovery — Western vendors’ 
increasing focus on integrated AIDC infrastructure strengthens the rationale for 
outsourcing select UPS platforms. Voltronic’s customization capabilities and 
flexible manufacturing position it to capture additional opportunities. A rising 
contribution from online and higher power UPS should support margins, driving EPS 
CAGR of 20% in 2026-2028E. 
Power racks could materially broaden its growth opportunity — Voltronic’s high 
voltage EV-charger experience and longstanding relationships with western power 
vendors support its entry into HVDC power solutions. It has progressed into 
customer sampling, with management indicating that HVDC power rack mass 
production could begin in 2H27E at the earliest. Successful commercialization could 
broaden its addressable market and support a valuation re-rating.
Earnings recovery and strong cash returns  — We view 2026E as the earnings 
trough, followed by a recovery in 2027-2028E. A richer online UPS mix and operating 
leverage should lift GPM from 27.1% in 2026E to 28.1% and 29.0% in 2027E and 
2028E, respectively. Its relatively asset-light business model, net cash position and 
high dividend payout should provide additional support for shareholder returns.
Initiate Buy with TP NT$1,050 on 25x 2027E EPS — Our target multiple is broadly 
consistent with Voltronic’s average forward P/E since 2013, supported by its

### Page 4

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Executive Summary
We initiate coverage of Voltronic with a Buy rating and target price of NT$1,050
(25x 2027E EPS), supported by structural UPS outsourcing, improving product mix
and emerging opportunities in AI datacenter power infrastructure. Voltronic is a
Taiwan-based ODM of uninterrupted power supplies (“UPS”) and power
conversion products for global brands. UPS system provides backup electricity and

protection against power disturbances, helping critical equipment maintain
operation during supply disruptions. UPS accounted for 63% of Voltronic’s 2025
group sales. Its pure-play ODM model, customization capabilities and
longstanding customer relationships underpin its competitive positioning.
Our positive stance on the shares is based on three major reasons:
1. We see expansion into HVDC power racks as a potentially significant new
growth opportunity. Its experience in high-voltage EV chargers provides

relevant engineering capabilities, while its established relationships with
western power vendors support access to customer development programs.
Voltronic has progressed into customer sampling, with management
indicating that HVDC power rack mass production could begin in 2H27E at the
earliest. Successful commercialization could broaden its addressable market
and support a valuation re-rating, although meaningful earnings contribution
remains subject to qualification, design wins and production schedules.

2. Structural UPS outsourcing should support sustained market share gains.
Western power vendors are allocating more resources to integrated AIDC
infrastructure and energy management, strengthening the rationale for
outsourcing selected products. Voltronic’s engineering expertise, flexible
manufacturing and ability to handle a broad range of customized products in
small batches make it well positioned to capture these opportunities.
3. The shift toward online and higher power UPS should improve revenue quality

and profitability. We expect online UPS to be the principal driver of the
earnings recovery, with online UPS sales growing 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E as
outsourcing opportunities and demand for higher power systems expand.
We view 2026E as the earnings trough, followed by a recovery in 2027-2028E. We
forecast EPS of NT$33.00/NT$42.30/NT$47.25 in 2026E/2027E/2028E, implying
earnings growth of 28% and 12% in 2027E and 2028E. A richer online UPS mix and

operating leverage should lift GPM from 27.1% in 2026E to 28.1% and 29.0% in
2027E and 2028E, respectively. Its relatively asset-light business model, net cash
position and high dividend payout should provide additional support for
shareholder returns.
Our target price applies 25x forward P/E to 2027E EPS, broadly consistent with
Voltronic’s long tern average forward P/E since 2013. We believe its competitive
position in UPS outsourcing, durable customer relationships and manufacturing

capabilities justify this multiple, with clearer evidence of power rack
commercialization offering potential for further re-rating.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research

Investment Thesis
Positive #1 – AIDC creates a new growth runway, with
power-rack expansion potentially lifting Voltronic into
broader power solutions
We see Voltronic’s expansion into power racks as a potentially significant extension
of its AIDC opportunity in the medium to long term, enabling the company to
capture the broader share of datacenter power spending. We believe Voltronic’s
existing high-voltage engineering capabilities, established relationships with

western power vendors and customer collaboration provide a credible foundation
for winning business in HVDC power rack.
Rapid growth in AI workloads and rising rack density increase the power capacity
required, alongside more demanding requirements for efficiency, reliability and
load management. Nvidia’s roadmap targets 800VDC infrastructure supporting
racks of 1MW and beyond, while its latest architecture includes power racks that
supply 800VDC to compute racks within existing AC-powered facilities. We believe

this creates scope for greater power-infrastructure, even as the architecture
consolidates conversion stages to improve efficiency.
EV-charger experience provides a relevant technical foundation for HVDC
development
Voltronic already offers DC fast chargers ranging 30-240kW, with modular designs
delivering constant power across a 300-100VDC range and charging controls
developed internally. These products demonstrate existing experience with high-

voltage conversion, protection, thermal management and modular power-system
design. We believe these capabilities should support its development of 800VDC
PSUs and reduce the engineering learning curve.
Established westerns customer relationships strengthen its route to market
Voltronic’s UPS ODM business provides existing engineering relationships and
familiarity with customers’ quality, customization and manufacturing
requirements. Its pure ODM business model also emphasizes avoiding competition

with customers. We believe this positioning makes it a suitable partner for western
vendors who are expanding their AI infrastructure offerings. Existing relationships
should help Voltronic secure development opportunities and understand product
roadmaps early, although each new platform still requires qualification.
Voltronic has already taken concrete steps toward this opportunity. The company
has publicly introduced an HVDC PSU for power racks, while its broader

development plan extends into solutions combining battery backup unit (BBUs).
battery management systems (BMS), and DCDC converters through collaboration
with power-system companies.
Management indicated discussions with two Tier 1 vendors with the intended
solution ultimately servicing the service providers. This creates a potential path
from supplying individual power products to participating in a larger, integrated
power-rack offering.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 9

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
9
Development has progressed into customer sampling, although specifications
continue to evolve with customers’ requirements. Management pointed out that
shipment of 800VDC products possible from 2H27E at the earliest.
We view its existing relationships with global power-solution vendors as a key
advantage, while its commitment to operating as a pure ODM without competing
against customers under its own brand supports this partnership model.

That said, customer qualification, confirmed design wins and mass-production
timing remain key milestones before we can confidently evaluate a material
contribution. Sales potential also depends on Voltronic’s scope of supply, which
determines content per rack and the share of system value it captures. Progress
toward commercial shipments could strengthen earnings visibility and support a
re-rating as Voltronic establishes its position in AIDC power infrastructure.

Positive #2 – UPS outsourcing provides a structural share-
gain opportunity

We see structural outsourcing as a durable growth driver for Voltronic, allowing its
UPS business to outgrow underlying industry demand. Rising investment in digital
infrastructure, commercial applications and industrial automation supports
continued demand for power protection, while established brands have incentives
to outsource selected hardware platforms.
Voltronic’s dedicated ODM model, engineering capabilities and manufacturing
efficiency position it to capture this opportunity. In our view, growth can come from

both an expanding market and the transfer of existing production from customers’
factories to qualified manufacturing partners.
UPS remains a structurally growing power-protection market
Global Market Insights estimates the worldwide UPS market at US$13bn in 2025,
rising to US$14.5bn in 2026E and US$26.8bn by 2035E, implying a 7.1% CAGR
over 2026-2035E. Although estimates vary by research provider and market
definition, the underlying drivers include increasing dependency on electronic

systems, growth in industrial automation and smart manufacturing, increasing
power outages and grad instability, rising costs of downtime and greater
requirements for reliable electricity.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 11

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
11
Datacenters provide an important source of incremental demand, while
commercial and industrial applications broaden the market beyond the AI
investment cycle. Datacenters expansion supports demand for online, double
conversion UPS systems, which continuously condition incoming power and
protect critical equipment against supply disturbance. Commercial applications –
including offices, retail networks and healthcare facilities – require protection for

servers, communications equipment and other essential systems. Industrial
demand comes from protecting sensitive control, automation equipment and
critical processes where power interruptions can disrupt production.
The competitive landscape is led by established international suppliers. Per Global
Market Insights 2025 analysis, Schneider Electric, Eaton, Vertiv are the three
leading suppliers, with the top 5 collectively controlling approximately 60-65% of

the global UPS market. Other peers include Huawei, KSTAR, Kehua, Delta and
Riello.
This concentration matters for the outsourcing thesis – securing additional projects
from a relatively small number of large brands can open substantial manufacturing
opportunities.

Western brands’ strategic priorities support further selective outsourcing

Per our industry checks, major UPS brands still manufacture a substantial portion
of their UPS products internally, while increasingly moving toward energy
management and committing resources to broader AIDC infrastructure. This could
result in small and medium UPS products to be gradually outsourced as this
transition develops. We view this as a multi-year opportunity, particularly where
established platforms can be transferred to qualified ODMs while customers retain

control over product specifications, branding and distribution.
We identify three economic reasons for this shift –
1.Outsourcing can improve manufacturing and capital efficiency: A specialist
ODM can aggregate demand across customers, consolidate component
purchases and spread manufacturing overhead across a larger production
base. For brands, this can reduce incremental factory investment and simplify
capacity planning.
2.Outsourcing can accelerate product development: access to ODMs’

established platforms, component knowledge and manufacturing processes
allows brands to adapt existing designs for additional markets and
applications. Engineering resources can then concentrate on product
differentiation.
3.Broader system offerings create competing demands on vendors’ resources.
Leading UPS brands emphasize on monitoring, operational management and
integrated solutions, strengthening the rationale for outsourcing selected
hardware while allocating more resources to system integration, and software.

Voltronic’s dedicated ODM model, engineering capabilities and manufacturing
efficiency should position it to capture further UPS outsourcing

Voltronic’s ODM focus is evident from its commitment to operate without having a
brand of its own to compete with its customers. This aligns its interests with
customers and differentiates it from suppliers that combine branded sales with
contract manufacturing. Long-standing relationships with leading vendors,
including Schneider, Riello, Vertiv and Eaton, provide a foundation for securing

additional projects. Its engineering capabilities enable it to meet diverse product
specifications, while flexible manufacturing supports the small production batches
and broad model range characteristic of the UPS industry.
Its track record in product qualification and certification also creates barriers, as
moving established programs to other suppliers requires additional validation,
audits and testing. We believe this combination of customer trust, execution and

cost competitiveness positions Voltronic to secure more outsourcing orders,
including higher-power online UPS, as western brands allocate greater resources
to AIDC infrastructure and integrated power solutions.

Positive #3 – Online and high-power UPS mix upgrade
should support structurally better growth
We expect Voltronic’s shift toward online and higher-power UPS to become an
increasingly important driver of revenue growth and profitability. Moving up the

product range increases power capacity and engineering content per system,
supporting higher ASPs and greater differentiation. We forecast online UPS sales to
rise at a 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E, ahead of offline UPS at 6%, with online UPS
gross profit growing faster at 16% as product mix improves.
Offline UPS remains a stable revenue base, yet offers more limited differentiation.
Offline UPS typically serves home offices, small businesses and basic power

applications. These products feature relatively simple designs and lower costs.
We view this segment as increasingly mature and commoditized, with lower
technical barriers exposing suppliers to greater pricing pressure, including
competition from Chinese manufacturers. Scale, procurement efficiency and cost
control hence are the key competitive factors, while opportunities for sustained
ASP and margin expansion are more constrained, in our view. Our model assumes

offline UPS gross margin to be at 24-26% in 2025-2028E.
Online UPS offers greater technical differentiation and stronger margin potential.
Its double-conversion architecture continuously converts AC power into DC and
back into AC, providing stable voltage and frequency, avoiding power disturbances
and enabling zero transfer time to battery operation.
It is particularly suited to complex, power-sensitive applications such as
datacenters, industrial automation and precision control systems, where even brief

interruptions or power quality disturbances can disrupt operations. The business
also exhibits high-mix, low-volume characteristics, particularly in customized
Schneider, 18%

### Page 15

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
15
commercial and industrial applications, where customers require different power
ratings, redundancy configuration and battery interfaces etc. Our model assumes
online UPS gross margin to be at 31-34% in 2025-2028E.
We believe this creates opportunities for an experienced ODM partner such as
Voltronic to differentiate through customization and reliable delivery, while sharing
development and procurement resources across customers and product platforms.

T ogether with demanding certification, customer qualification and reliability
testing, these capabilities raise entry barriers and switching costs, supporting
higher ASPs, strong customer retention and better margin potential as Voltronic
expands into higher power systems.
We project online UPS sales to increase 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E, lifting its
contribution to group sales from 37% in 2025 to c.44% in 2028E. The underlying
driver comes from a stronger outsourcing opportunity thanks to the AIDC boom.

Western vendors, including Schneider, Vertiv are expanding their focus on
integrated AIDC infrastructure, power distribution, and infrastructure
management. Meanwhile, rising power requirements within conventional AC-
based datacenters support demand for higher power UPS systems, prompting
vendors to expand UPS production capacity. We believe the combination of
stronger demand and competing resource requirements can strain available
engineering and manufacturing capabilities, increasing the appeal of qualified

ODM partners to supplement internal production. Voltronic’s customization
capabilities and flexible manufacturing position it to capture this opportunity.

### Page 17

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
17
Financial Analysis
Income statement – 2026E marks the earnings trough; online UPS drives
the recovery from 2027E
Voltronic delivered an 8.7% sales CAGR and 9.9% net profit CAGR over 2018-2025,
although earnings have become more volatile in recent years due largely to swings
in the inverter business. We forecast sales to decrease 1% y/y to NT$20.4bn in
2026E, before recovering 8% y/y and 7% y/y to NT$21.9bn and NT$23.4bn in

2027E and 2028E respectively. The key driver is the higher value online UPS.
We forecast online UPS sales to grow 10%/13%/10% y/y in 2026E/2027E/2028E,
lifting its sales contribution from 37% in 2025 to 44% by 2028E. T ogether with
offline UPS, we estimate UPS products to make up 72% of group sales in 2028E vs
2025’s 63%. Conversely, we forecast inverter sales to decline 27% y/y in 2026E and
remain broadly flat thereafter, reducing their sales contribution from 29% in 2025

to 18% in 2028E.
We expect gross margin to bottom at 27.1% in 2026E vs. 29.0% in 2025, reflecting
negative impact from material cost inflation and weaker inverter profitability.
Thereafter, we forecast gross margin to recover to 28.1% and 29.0% in 2027E and
2028E, supported by a richer online UPS mix and improving segment margins. We
estimate online UPS gross margin to increase from 30.8% in 2026E to 33.4% in
2028E driven rising contribution from high power online UPS, while its rising sales

mix could provide increasing contribution to group gross profits.
Operating leverage should further support profitability. Opex fell from 8.0% of sales
in 2025 to 7.4% in 2026E and could remain at 7.2% thereafter, while R&D
investment is maintained at 4.2% of sales. We therefore forecast OPM to recover
from 19.7% in 2026E to 20.9% and 21.8% in 2027E and 2028E.
We estimate net profit to decrease 18% y/y in 2026E, partly hampered by a larger
FX loss, before rebounding 28% y/y and 12% y/y in 2027E and 2028E. We forecast

EPS to register NT$33.00/NT$42.30/NT$47.25 in 2026E/2027E/2028E.
We attribute Voltronic’s earnings decline in 2025 and our forecast for a further
decline in 2026E to persistent inverter weakness and additional cost and supply
pressures. In 2025, adverse weather in Pakistan shortened the inverter peak
season and weakened demand, while intensifying Chinese competition pressured
pricing and profitability. The weakness extended into 2026E as the Middle East

conflict disrupted logistics and delayed shipments, alongside continued customers
destocking in Pakistan. In 1H26, RMB appreciation further weighed on margins.
Meanwhile, raw material shortages constrained shipments and higher metal and
key component prices eroded gross margins. We therefore forecast earnings to
decline 18% y/y in 2026E before improving 28% y/y in 2027E.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Balance Sheet – Asset light model and net cash suggest high returns
Voltronic maintains a relatively asset-light balance sheet with fixed assets making
up 24% of total assets in 2025 despite its sizable sales scale. We estimate the

company to remain in a net cash position throughout 2026-2028E. We forecast
cash and cash equivalent assets to account for 36-39% of total assets in 2026-
2028E while there are no meaningful short-term borrowings and long-term debt.
We forecast ROE to rise from 37% in 2025 to 40% in 2026E and 48% in 2027E. The
high ROE is not driven by financial leverage, but primarily by its asset-light
business model, strong operating margins and high capital distribution.

Voltronic also maintains high shareholder-return policy. We assume an 87% cash
dividend payout ratio through 2026-2028E, implying cash dividends of
NT$29/NT$37/NT$41 per share.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 25x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1,050, broadly in line with its long-term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s established
earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating leverage to its
profitability. Using 2027E EPS captures a fuller year of anticipated earnings

recovery following the softer 2026E base, alongside the ongoing transition toward
online and higher power UPS. This also aligns our valuation with the earnings
outlook investors are likely to focus on as the recovery progresses.
Our target multiple of 25x is supported by Voltronic’s competitive position in UPS
outsourcing. Its established relationship with western power vendors and ability to
accommodate high mix, low volume production creates a solid foundation for the

intact share gains, supporting the durability of its earnings and hence justify a
valuation broadly consistent with its historical average. Stronger evidence of power
rack commercialization and sustained UPS mix improvement could support a
further re-rating.

Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include:
1.Slower UPS demand or outsourcing gain. Weaker commercial and industrial
spending, delayed datacenter projects or customer inventory adjustments
could weigh on orders.
2.  Less favorable product mix and margin pressure. A slower transition toward
online and high-power UPS, more aggressive pricing or higher component
costs could limit margin improvement.

3. Persistent PV inverter competition. Aggressive pricing by Chinese suppliers,
weaker residential solar demand or unfavorable subsidy changes could
pressure inverter sales and profitability.
Our quantitative model assigns a High Risk rating to Voltronic. However, we
believe such a rating is not warranted due to: 1) its established franchise and
longstanding relationships with leading power vendors, with demanding
qualification requirements and high customization supporting customer retention

and repeat business; 2) its track record of profitability and positive operating cash
flow enables internally funded investment and reduces reliance on external
financing; and 3) the company has a strong balance sheet and has been in net cash
position.

### Page 24

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
24
Company Overview
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014, Voltronic
Power is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. The company is headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing
facilities in Taiwan, China and Vietnam, with approximately 5,000 employees.

Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model and does not market products
under its own brand. This avoids direct competition with customers and has helped
the company establish long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS
brands, as well as numerous regional brand owners.
The company provides customers with product design, R&D, sourcing,
manufacturing and system integration services, with a focus on high-mix,
relatively low-volume and customized products.

Voltronic’s core products include offline UPS systems ranging from 400VA to 3kVA
and on-line UPS systems ranging from 1kVA to 3MVA. The company has
progressively expanded from conventional single phase UPS products into three-
phase, high-power and modular UPS systems. It has also diversified into off-grid
and hybrid solar inverters, energy storage systems, and EV chargers.
In 2025, online UPS contributed 37% of group sales, offline UPS 26%, inverters

29% and others 8%. By geo, Asia accounted for 60% of group sales, followed by
Americas 22%, EU 16% and others 2%.

Shareholder Structure
As of March 2026, founder, chairman and general manager Alex Hsieh was the

largest individual shareholder, holding 9.6% of outstanding shares. Based on
disclosed direct, family-related and investment company holdings, we estimate
the founder and related parties collectively controlled c.30% of outstanding
shares.
Key Management Team
nAlex Hsieh – Founder, Chairman and General Manager: Mr. Hsieh has led
Voltronic since its establishment in 2008. He holds a bachelor’s degree in
business management from Tatung University and previously served as

Chairman and General Manager of Centralion Industrial and as a director of
Phoenixtec Power. His extensive experience in the UPS industry underpins
Voltronic’s pure-play ODM strategy.
nMonica Wang – Vice General Manager: Ms. Wang joined Voltronic at its
establishment in 2008. She previously worked as a manager at Centralion
Industrial.
nChih-Hsin Chin – Vice General Manager: Mr. Chin joined Voltronic in 2008.
Before joining the company, he worked as a manager at Phoenixtec Power.

### Page 28

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
28
Voltronic Power
Company description
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014,
Voltronic is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model, which avoids
direct competition with customers and has helped the company establish

long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS brands. The company is
headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing facilities in Taiwan,
China and Vietnam.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Voltronic, supported by an earnings recovery,
structural UPS outsourcing and emerging opportunities in AIDC power
infrastructure. Its customization capabilities and longstanding relationships
with western power vendors position it to capture additional outsourcing

orders and expand into higher power products. HVDC power racks offer a L-T
growth opportunity and potential re-rating catalyst. We view 2026E as
earnings trough, with a richer online UPS mix and operating leverage driving a
28% earnings rebound in 2027E.
Valuation
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 25x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1050, broadly in line with its long term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s

established earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating
leverage to its profitability.
Risks
Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include: 1) Slower UPS
demand or outsourcing gain. 2) Less favorable product mix and margin
pressure. 3) Persistent PV inverter competition. 4) Weaker macro economy

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

動態更新 IC製造 ‧ 台灣

台積電 
(2330.TW /2330 TT)

多、更多、再更多的 N3 產能擴張 
重要訊息 
台積電 3Q26 財報表現符合預期，管理層預估 2026 年營收將年增 44%。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 符合預期，4Q26 展望強勁。3Q26 毛利率 66.4%，受 N2 量產爬坡稀
釋影響，較我們預估低 1.6 個百分點，但符合公司財測。受惠營收成長及營
業費用控管，營業利益及營益率均符合預期。台積電 4Q26 營收財測為 523
億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增 14%，較我們先前預估高出 7%，毛
利率及營益率則預期持平，主要受惠 HPC 需求強勁及 N2 量產爬坡。 
N3 持續擴產，並評估美國亞利桑那州以外的擴產機會 。隨 4Q26 展望轉
強，台積電將 2026 年美元營收成長預估由原先 40%以上上調至 44%，並
維持全年資本支出 600 億至 640 億美元。台積電過去鮮少將單一製程產能
擴充至超過原先規劃的目標( 約每月 15 萬片)。然而，除近期宣布的三座晶圓
廠(台南 Fab 18 、亞利桑那州二廠及熊本二廠) 外，台積電再宣布於台南新增
一座 N3 晶圓廠，預計於 1H28 量產。整體而言，我們預估 N3 產能將由
2026 年底的每月 15 萬至 16 萬片，在未來兩年進一步增加 8 萬片，增幅達
50%。我們認為此次擴產反映三項需求：(1)Agentic AI 帶動的 CPU 需求；
(2)HBM4e 客製化基底晶粒；以及(3)AI ASIC 。此外，台積電亦正評估在美國
亞利桑那州以外地區擴產的可能性，例如德州，但目前尚未做出最終決定。  
不會將任何商機拱手讓給競爭對手 。管理層多次強調不會讓競爭對手取得任
何商機，體現在 N3 產能的積極擴充。此外，儘管我們預期 Google(美 )委託
Intel(美 )生產的 Humufish 目前沒有備援計劃。然而，在必要情況下，台積電
將協助客戶確保產品如期交付， 暗示若 Intel 的 EMIB-T 製程進度落後，台積
電有望進一步提升市占率。部分投資人可能擔憂台積電導入 ASML High -NA
設備的時程較競爭對手晚兩年，進而影響其技術領先地位。但我們持不同看
法，採用最新設備並不必然代表技術領先。若 在 2030 年前台積電能在不投
資昂貴設備的情況下達到相近的製程效能，反而 有望取得相較同業更顯著的
成本優勢。 
投資建議  
儘管 3Q26 財報符合預期，且我們預估 4Q26 表現將更為強勁，但在市場預
期極高下，投資人仍可能對短期毛利率走勢有所疑慮。考量未來的成長動能
及穩健的執行能力，我們建議投資人趁股價回檔時逢低布局。我們僅微幅調
整獲利預估， 但隨評價基準遞延至 2028 年，我們 將目標價上調至 3,650
元，仍基於景氣上行週期平均的 20 倍本益比，維持「增加持股」評等 。 
投資風險  
全球景氣不確定性；積極擴產導致供給過剩。  
增加持股‧維持

法說會重點數據 
 4Q26 財測： 營收預估為 523 億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增
14%；毛利率 65-67%，營益率 56.5- 58.5%。 
 2026 年展望：全年營收成長預估上調至年增 44%，維持資本支出 600
億至 640 億美元。  
 毛利率稀釋：海外擴產預計稀釋 2026 年毛利率 2 個百分點，未來幾年
稀釋幅度將擴大至 3-4 個百分點。  
 產能規劃：  
 N2 製程家族產能預計於 2027 年增加至兩倍以上。  
 台南新增 N3 晶圓廠預計於 1H27 量產，另一座 N3 晶圓廠則規劃於
1H28 量產。 
 亞利桑那州 N3 二廠已完成廠房建設，目前正進行設備搬入及安
裝，預計於 3Q27 量產，略早於原訂時程。  
 日本 N3 製程預計於 2028 年量產。 
 A14 製程已開始進行產能準備，預計於 2027 年如期試產，並於
2028 年量產。

台灣 台積電

十月 15, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 6

ESG 圖表定義  
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力(公用事業為出售而生產) 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者(太陽能、風能、水能、生
物質能、地熱能)之量 
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2約當排放量 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

CO2約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸)銷售(百萬元)比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因(自願或非自願)離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 = (離開之員工/平均員工人數)*100 
- 平均員工人數 = (本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數)/2 
- 本財年末員工數 = 上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練(如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展) 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1天 = 8小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv，凱基

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
Takeaways on Photonics from
2026 OCP Global Summit
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Betty Chen
Research Associate
Betty.H.Chen@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7213
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line

On Day 3 of the 2026 OCP Global Summit, there is a full-day special focus on
Photonics. Industry participants such as NVIDIA, Meta, Microsoft, Oracle, Broadcom,
Celestica, Arista, Ciena, Amphenol, Lumentum, and Huawei presented their views.
We summarize the key findings below:
1) When the moderator asks key industry players whether they will apply CPO or
NPO, almost all companies answered 'Both'.
2) AI density depends on optical connectivity. With the day's focus on Photonics, the

importance of optical connectivity was well highlighted in each session.
3) Five key metrics for new optical technology: 1) performance, 2) power, 3) cost, 4)
speed to deployment and 5) reliability and serviceability. Huawei believes NPO has
advantages on maintainability, open decoupling, availability and industry
implementation.
4) Scale-in is another new approach to enhance AI density.
5) Root cause of CPO failures: 83-85% of failures due to ELS, 11-17% due to fiber/

connector. Dust and dirt are also common failure issues.
6) Optics vs. Copper: Full Optical Pod is targeted towards scale-up sizes >576
accelerators, Optics and Copper to coexist between 72 and 576 accelerators.
Greater China Technology Hardware: Takeaways on NPO from 2026 OCP Global
Summit (14 Oct 2026)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
A full day discussion of Photonics further demonstrated the importance of
optical connectivity.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

3Q26 營收 持 續 成 長 ， 財 務 營 運 槓 桿 與 獲 利 結 構 持 續 優 化：

高 價 值 市 場 銷 量 顯 著 成 長 ， 團 隊 擴 編 與 精 準 醫 學 行 銷 策 略 效 益
大 舉 顯 現。毛 利 率 94.2 3%，受 惠 美 國 5M2 6 調升藥價 ，以 及 CMS
回 沖， 營 業 費 用 33.00 億元 (YoY +60.68%) ，增 加 主 因 延 續 團 隊

多 元 ， 增 幅 遠 低 於 營 收 成 長， 展 現 高 營 運 槓 桿 之 強 勁 爆 發 力。  
ET 新 適 應 症 在 全 球 步 入 收 割 期 ， 加 上 PMF 收 案 順 利 與 肝 癌 術
後 100% 防 復 發 數 據 ， 中 長 期 佈 局 完 整：  
原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 為 過 去 30 年 來 缺 乏 新 藥 之 領 域，公 司
於 05/12/2026 率 先 取 得 台 灣 ET 藥 證 ； 日 本 MHLW 專 家 會 議 於
07/2026 通 過 審 查 後 ， 已 於 08/24/2026 正 式 獲 日 本 核 准 用 於 所
有成人 ET 患者 ， 包 括 未 曾 接 受 治 療 者 ； 隨 後 美 國 FDA 亦 正 式
核 准 用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 成 為 近 30 年首款 ET 新 藥 並 具 7
年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔 期；中 國 與 韓 國 則 分 別 預 計 於 11/2026 及 12/2026
取 證 ， 並 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申 請 。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 15 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
因 應 企 業 永 續 經 營 所 帶 來 的 挑 戰 與 機 會，整 合 各 方 利 害 關 係 人 關 注 之 議 題，
將 展 開 「 永 續 經 營(ESG) 計 畫 」 ， 成 立 企 業 永 續 發 展 委 員 會 ， 聚 焦 於 五 大 策
略 ， 連 結 聯 合 國 「 永 續 發 展 目 標 SDGs 」 ， 以 企 業 核 心 精 神 與 專 業 ， 落 實 永
續 經 營 各 項 目 標，促 進 永 續 的 經 濟 成 長、社 會 發 展 與 環 境 保 護，提 升 企 業 競
爭 力 與 發 揮 影 響 力 。 
公 司 簡 介 ： 
藥華藥主要致力於研發和生產新一代創新型生物藥，特別聚焦於血液腫瘤
領 域 。 公 司 旗 艦 產 品 為 Ropeginterferon alfa -2b ( 簡稱 Ropeg ，商品名
Besremi) ， 是 一 個 創 新 的 長 效 型 干 擾 素 藥 物 ， 目 前 已 在 美 國 、 歐 洲 、 日 本 、
韓 國 、 台 灣 及 中 國 等 全 球 約 50 個 國 家 取 得 藥 證 ， 用 於 治 療 成 人 真 性 紅 血 球
增多症 (Polycythemia Vera, PV) ， 並 已 陸 續 於 台 灣 、 日 本 及 美 國 取 得 原 發 性
血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 藥 證 。  
Ropeg 是 藥 華 藥 自 行 研 發、並 擁 有 全 球 獨 家 權 利 的 新 藥。公 司 利 用 其 特 有 的
長 效 型 蛋 白 質 藥 物 技 術 平 台 ， 成 功 開 發 出Ropeg 。 過 去 PV 的 治 療 藥 物 有 限
且 常 伴 隨 副 作 用 ， 包 含 老 藥 HU 及 尚 未 獲 PV 適 應 症 批 准 的 干 擾 素 藥 物 ， 與
美 國 藥 廠 Incyte 開 發 的 Jakafi (ruxolitinib) 。 2019 年， Ropeg 獲 得 歐 洲 藥 品
管理局 (EMA) 核 准 上 市 ， 並 在 2021 年 11 月 獲 得 美 國 FDA 的 核 准 ， 成 為 第
一個被 FDA 批 准 用 於 所 有 PV 成 人 的 治 療 藥 物 ， 不 受 之 前 治 療 史 的 限 制 ，
也 是 第 一 個 被 FDA 核 准 特 別 用 於 PV 的 干 擾 素 療 法 ， 且 已 連 續 四 年 獲NCCN
治 療 指 引 推 薦 。 
相 較 於 傳 統 干 擾 素 ，Ropeg 具 有 更 長 的 半 衰 期 ， 因 此 可 以 延 長 給 藥 間 隔 ， 提
高 病 患 的 便 利 性 及 順 從 性，同 時 也 展 現 出 更 優 異 的 療 效。 在 7.3 年 的 臨 床 追
蹤 數 據 顯 示 76% 的 完 全 血 液 學 反 應 率(CHR) ， 並 能 持 續 降 低 JAK2 基 因 突 變
負 擔 。 藥 華 藥 在 美 國 、 歐 洲 、 日 本 及 台 灣 等 地 設 有 子 公 司 ， 負 責 藥 物 的 銷
售 與 推 廣 。Ropeg 目 前 是 公 司 最 主 要 的 營 收 來 源 ， 佔 比 超 過9 成 。 此 外 也 積
極拓展 Ropeg 的 其 他 適 應 症 ， 包 含 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 及 骨 髓 纖 維 化(MF) 等。。 
3Q26 營 收 季 增，9 月 未 含 CMS 調 整 仍 創 新 高，財 務 營 運 槓 桿 與 獲 利 結 構 持
續 優 化 ：  
2Q2 6 營收 68. 55 億元 (YoY+90.30%) ， 主 要 源 於 美 國 與 日 本 兩 大 高 價 值 市 場
銷 量 顯 著 成 長 ， 團 隊 擴 編 與 精 準 醫 學 行 銷 策 略 效 益 大 舉 顯 現 。 毛 利 率
94.2 3%， 受 惠 美 國 5M2 6 調 升 藥 價，以及 CMS 回沖， 營 業 費 用 33.00 億元
(YoY +60.68%) ， 增 加 主 因 延 續 團 隊 擴 編 、 商 業 推 廣 以 及 依 會 計 準 則 認 列 限
制 員 工 權 利 新 股 約 2 億多元 ，增 幅 遠 低 於 營 收 成 長，展 現 高 營 運 槓 桿 之 強 勁
爆發力 。  
ET 新 適 應 症 在 全 球 步 入 收 割 期 ， 併 購 予 宇 切 入 新 興 療 法 ， 加 上PMF 收案
順 利 與 肝 癌 術 後 100% 防 復 發 數 據 ， 中 長 期 佈 局 完 整：  
原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 為過去 30 年 來 缺 乏 新 藥 之 領 域 ， 公 司 於05/12/2026
率 先 取 得 台 灣 ET 藥 證 ， 用 於 HU 抗 藥 性 或 無 法 耐 受 之 成 人 ET 病 人 ； 日 本
MHLW 專 家 會 議 於 07/2026 通 過 審 查 ， 破 例 同 意 將 全 球 三 期 SURPASS -ET
與 北 美 單 臂 二 期 XC 數 據 合 併 評 估 ， 並 已 於 08/24/2026 正 式 獲 日 本 核 准 用

3 群益投顧 
於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 包 括 未 曾 接 受 治 療 者 ； 隨 後 美 國FDA 亦 正 式 核 准 Ropeg
用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 成 為 近 30 年首款 ET 新 藥 ， 並 具 7 年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔
期，配 合 芝 加 哥 誓 師 大 會 已 完 成 之 團 隊 培 訓 與 推 廣 文 宣，取 證 後 已 立 即 展 開
商 業 推 廣 ； 其 他 市 場 方 面 ， 中 國 大 陸 與 韓 國ET 審 查 同 步 進 行 ， 分 別 預 計 於
11/2026 及 12/2026 取 證 ， 同 時 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申
請。早 期 原 發 性 骨 髓 纖 維 化(PMF) 三 期 臨 床 HOPE -PMF 於 06/2026 完 成 收 案
且 超 收 至 160 人 (原目標 150 人 )， 預 計 2027 年 完 成 試 驗 、2028 年 中 申 請 藥
證。  
在 實 體 腫 瘤 領 域 ，B 型 肝 炎 相 關 肝 癌(HCC) 術 後 防 復 發 一 期 試 驗 ，Ropeg 序
列合併 Anti -PD1 在 15 名 患 者 中 位 追 蹤 1,024 天 ， 創 下 100% 無 復 發 存 活 率
(RFS) 之 重 大 突 破 。PEG -IL -2 (P11838) 、 PD -1-IL -2v 、 ADC 及 KRAS TCR -T
將於 2026 -2027 年 陸 續 申 請 IND 。  
藥 華 藥 近 期 斥 資 約 1.68 億 元 收 購 予 宇 生 技 100% 股 權 ， 取 得 脂 質 奈 米 顆 粒
(LNP) 藥 物 遞 送 平 台 、 研 發 設 備 及 專 業 團 隊 ， 並 納 入 兩 項 新 藥 研 發 專 案 。 予
宇成立於 2019 年， LNP 平 台 涵 蓋 配 方 設 計 、 微 流 體 製 程 與 分 析 方 法 ， 可 支
援 mRNA 、 saRNA 及 circRNA 等 多 元 RNA 藥 物 開 發 ； 原 有 FR -202 與 FR -
201 兩 項 專 案 分 別 鎖 定 端 粒 生 物 學 疾 病 與 威 爾 森 氏 症 ， 預 計 於 2027 年及
2028 年 提 出 IND 。  
此 項 收 購 對 公 司 營 運 具 三 大 正 面 意 義 ：(1) 收 購 金 額 僅 約 1.68 億 元 ， 相 對 公
司單季逾 25 億 元 淨 利 規 模 ， 財 務 負 擔 極 低 ；(2) 取 得 遞 送 系 統 自 主 能 力 ， 可
與公司既有蛋白質藥物製造資源整合，從研發早期即同步規劃藥物設計與
後 續 製 造 ；(3) 延 伸 研 發 版 圖 至 In Vivo CAR -T 此 一 國 際 大 廠 積 極 布 局 的 領
域 ， 提 升 中 長 期 選 擇 權 價 值 ， 亦 為 後 續 研 發 產 品 線 布 局 增 加 深 度 與 廣 度 。 
注 射 筆 上 市 與 潛 力 患 者 數 上 修 雙 輪 驅 動，配 合 真 實 世 界 數 據 防止 惡 化，鞏 固
Ropeg 在 PV 領 域 之 地 位 ：  
Ropeg 在 PV 適 應 症 已 於 全 球 約 50 個 國 家 獲 批 ， 加 拿 大 亦 於 07/02/2026 
順 利 取 證 。 美 國 FDA 於 06/2026 核准 Ropeg 注 射 筆 ， 並 於 07/2026 正 式 出
貨 開 賣，注 射 筆 具 備 極 細 針 頭、精 準 調 劑 及 一 次 性 自 動 縮 回 防 針 扎 設 計，短
期 可 加 速 替 換 傳 統 預 充 充 填 針 筒 Prefilled Syringe ， 中 長 期 將 提 升 患 者 長 期
用 藥 依 從 性 與 留 存 率 。 
武田製藥 (Takeda) 競品 Rusfertide (擬 似 鐵 調 素)預 計 於 08/2026 獲 批，藥 華 藥
指出  Rusfertide 僅 定 位 於 配 合 放 血 之 被 動 症 狀 控 制 ， 而 Ropeg 為 一 線 首 選
積 極 療 法 ， 真 實 世 界 數 據 證 實 Ropeg 合 併 治 療 可 使 患 者 疾 病 惡 化成 骨 髓 纖
維化  (MF )的 概 率 維 持 在 0%。最 新 流 行 病 學 文 獻 將 美 國 MPN 總 潛 力 患 者 數
上修至 34.70 萬人 ( 原 30.50 萬人 )， 競 品 進 入市 場 將 大 幅 提 升 早 期 治 療 之 病
識 感 與 診 斷 率 ， 反 而 有 助 於 Ropeg 進 一 步 擴 大 一 線 市 場 壟 斷 地 位 。 
藥 華 藥 與 AOP 仲 裁 案 ： 
關於 AOP 第 二 階 段 仲 裁 判 斷 ， 藥 華 藥 認 為 其 程 序 存 在 違 反 程 序 正 義 及 聽 審
權 之 重 大 瑕 疵 ， 目 前 正 與 外 部 律 師 團 隊 嚴 謹 評 估 ， 將 在 法 定 時 限 內 向 德 國
法 蘭 克 福 高 等 法 院 提 起 「 撤 銷 第 二 階 段 仲 裁 判 斷 之 訴 」 。 
由 於 法 律 程 序 仍 在 評 估 中 ， 現 階 段 無 法 判 斷 或 預 測 該 裁 定 金 額 發 生 的 時 間
點 ， 後 續 發 展 視 訴 訟 進 度 而 定 。 公 司 與AOP 之 合 約 目 前 仍 處 於 有 效 狀 態 ，

4 群益投顧 
對 於 任 何 有 助 於 妥 善 解 決 爭 議 、 維 護 股 東 權 益 之 方 案 均 持 開 放 態 度 。 歐 洲
仍 為 藥 華 藥 極 為 重 視 之 核 心 市 場 ， 將 綜 合 評 估 法 規 要 求 、 商 業 推 廣 與 契 約
關 係 ， 持 續 規 劃 並 評 估 於 歐 洲 申 請 ET 藥 證 之 可 行 性 。 
AOP 在 8/7/2026 發 布 的 官 方 新 聞 稿 表 示 ， 藥 華 藥 延 遲 造 成 的 損 害 約€82M ，
2019 -2022 過 高 價 格 所 產 生 的 退 款 約 €31M ， AOP 原 本 應 支 付 給 藥 華 藥 的
profit sharing 約 17M 可 用 來 抵 銷 ； 最 終 AOP 表 示 自 己 不 用 付 款 給 藥 華 藥 ，
並 認 為 藥 華 藥 目 前 對 AOP 的 責 任 已 進 入 「 三 位 數 百 萬 歐 元 」 範 圍 。 推 論 藥
華 藥 實 際 曝 險 金 額 Net Set -off 後 約 為 €97.48M (約新台幣 36 億元 )。  
AOP 提 出 延 伸 主 張 ， 若 將 仲 裁 庭 對 2019 -2022 年 價 格 爭 議( 最高 900% 超額
定 價 之 認 定)的 計 算 邏 輯 套 用 至 2023 -2025 年供貨， AOP 主 張 藥 華 藥 需 再 退
還約 €65M ， 此 項 目 僅 為 AOP 單 方 面 之 延 伸 主 張 ， 非 現 階 段 確 定 之 仲 裁 判
決。  
藥 華 藥 已 從 純 研 發 型 生 技 公 司 轉 型 為 具 備 強 勁 商 業 化 現 金 流 之 藥 廠 ， 一 次
性認列約 36 億 元 損 失 衝 擊 ， 不 至 於 構 成 營 運 生 存 危 機。

投 資 建 議：(1)07/2026 於 美 國 開 賣 Ropeg 注 射 筆 提 升 患 者 用 藥 順 應 性 與 留 存
率 ， 推 升 低 風 險 PV/ET 患 者 滲 透 率 ， 為 2H26 營 收 加 速 引 擎 ；(2)ET 新 適 應
症 迎 來 多 國 藥 證 收 割，開 啟 第 二 成 長 曲 線，台 灣、日本 取 證 後，美國 FDA 亦
核 准 用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 並 具 7 年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔 期，中 國 與 韓 國 分 別 預 計 於
11/2026 與 12/2026 取 證 ， 並 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申 請。
(3) 預估 2026 年 EPS 2 4.93 元、 2027 年 EPS 42. 45 元 ， 投 資 建 議 買 進 ， 目 標
價 1,6 00 元 。

7 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

泰合 (6467 TT) 
開花結果在即

永豐觀點 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議買進：泰合 10/20 上市掛牌，掛牌價105.6 元。由於(1) 泰合開發之口溶
膜劑型藥物有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點下，市場競爭力足；(2) 抗血栓口
溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，2027 年在授權與上市權利金開始認列下 ，營運轉
虧為盈且呈大跳躍狀；(3) 由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至 2028 年 4
月後才可上市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜
分市場。且因 BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其
獨特的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的價折壓力相對輕微，建議買進，目
標價 170 元( 18 X 2027 EPS )。

營運現況與分析 
公司簡介：泰合生技成立於 2010 年，專注開發 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥與特殊學名
藥，核心技術為自主研發的 TDS 透皮／透黏膜傳輸平台，將現有口服藥品成分改
造為透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型 ，提供方便使用且更安全的新劑型藥物。 產品開發
上，505(b)(2)部分，治療抗血栓口溶膜貼片(每日兩片)BEQIA 靜待歐美取證中外，
其餘則仍在臨床階段中，困難學名藥部分，化療止吐口溶膜2018 年日本取證銷售
中外，其餘四項藥品則在進行洽談授權合作。 
圖一：泰合產品線開發時程

資料來源：泰合；永豐投顧研究部整理，Oct. 2026

### Page 2

2

口溶膜劑型藥物能有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點 ：傳統口服錠劑藥物必須經
過食道、進入胃腸道消化，再經由肝臟代謝後才進入血液循環，泰合開發之口溶膜
劑型藥物， 可讓藥膜在接觸舌頭或口腔黏膜數秒內快速溶解 ， 病患完全不需飲水即
可完成給藥， 且部份藥物分子能直接透過口腔黏膜上皮細胞微血管吸收， 直接進入
全身血液循環， 不僅能提高藥物生體可用率， 還能減少胃酸對藥物的破壞與對胃腸
道的刺激下，有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點，提高患者服藥順從性。 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，提前卡位下，營運動能可期：泰合自主
開發的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥 BEQIA， 預計4Q26 取得歐盟藥證 、2Q27 獲美國
FDA 核准上市。目前該藥原廠在美國市場年銷售額>200 億美元，相較原廠口服錠
劑，泰合 BEQIA 具備三大競爭優勢：不需飲水（服藥便利）、解決吞嚥困難（減
輕照顧端壓力），以及攜帶與給藥便利（優化長照與醫療機構給藥流程）。 
市場競爭方面，由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至2028 年 4 月後才可上
市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜分市場。此
外，由於BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其獨特
的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的折價壓力應相對輕微。 
泰合海外市場營銷預計將採授權分潤模式，研究部預估 1H27 歐美地區簽約金應
有機會各達 500~1000 萬美金外，隨美國地區 2Q27 上市、通路鋪設及醫保陸續
准入，Peak sales 市佔率 2-3%(預期將落於 2029-30 年)、銷售分潤 12-15%估算
下，稅後 EPS 貢獻將落於 10-20 元。 
財務預估：預估泰合 2026 年營收 0.1 億元，稅後虧損 1.89 億元，稅後 EPS 以掛
牌後股本 5.99 億元估算，稅後 EPS-3.15 元，2027 年預估歐美地區授權簽約金各
500 萬美金及 1000 萬美金 ， 及上市銷售後權利金認列下 ，預估營收將跳升至7.75
億元，稅後淨利 5.68 億元，稅後 EPS 9.49 元。

泰合(6467)

32.25/302

泰合主力產品 BEQIA 專為吞嚥困難族群設計 ， 以Apixaban 為成分
的抗血栓口溶膜，鎖定高齡、吞嚥困難及需長期抗血栓治療族群 。
BEQIA 於 2026 年 6 月 22 日重新送件美國 FDA，進入 10 個月
PDUFA 審查期，2027 年 4 月 22 日為審核完成目標日期，可望於
2Q27 取得美國暫時性核准(Tentative Approval)。 歐洲EMA 方面 ，
則於 2026 年 7 月 17 日完成 Day 121 關鍵清單回覆 ， 朝CHMP 最
終審查意見(Day 210)與 EC 核准推進，公司預計 4Q26 可望獲歐盟
上市許可 。授權策略方面，泰合採取原廠與非原廠雙軌並行 。 對原廠
而言，BEQIA 可借力其成熟通路快速推升口溶模市占，並在面臨專
利懸崖與學名藥競爭時，可藉此維持利潤表現，洽談時即以解決原
廠現有困境的定位切入 。 至於非原廠 ， 泰合採精準區域分擊 ， 鎖定主
要市場以外的區域優先展開談判，累積市場實績與聲量。 泰合目前
已在歐美、紐澳、拉丁美洲等地區與數十家藥廠簽訂NDA，並持續
洽談，多家跨國藥廠與各區域BD 代表已進入實質洽談階段。此外，
下一代產品 TAH3341 長效口溶膜雖仍處臨床前，但也有廠商表達
興趣並關注發展進度 。泰合對外競價拍賣 5100 張 ， 競拍底價88 元，
最高投標張數 669 張，每股承銷價 105.6 元。評價上，參考同業有
6576 逸達以及 6838 台新藥，逸達生技近年股價區間 58-141 元，
股本 15.75 億元，市值區間 91.3-222 億元。台新藥近年股價區間
22-70 元，股本 15.1 億元，市值區間 33-106 億元。而泰合目前股
本 6 億元，參考同業市值區間，給予目標價 260 元，給予買進之投
資建議。

➢ 公司簡介： 
泰合生技成立於 2010 年 7 月，為新劑型新藥及特殊學名藥開發廠，藉由自
行開發之藥物傳輸平台技術結合現有藥物成份，將現有口服藥品成分改造為
透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型，以經皮吸收貼片與口溶膜藥品為代表 。公司專
注於開發 505(b)(2)NDA 新劑型新藥，並藉由 Transepithelial Delivery 
System (TDS)藥物傳輸技術平台改良現有藥物缺點，主要包括透皮及透黏膜
兩種不同藥物傳輸平台，劑型主要包含經皮吸收貼片、口溶膜與口頰溶膜三
類。產品線涵蓋抗血栓藥品、鴉片過量解毒及戒毒劑、小兒過動症、化療止
吐等治療領域。截至 2025 年，營收來自已授權之學名藥及受委託開發之專
案收入 99%。

抗凝血藥物市場: 
在全球醫療環境中，心血管疾病為主要致死與致殘原因之一，其發生率隨人
口老齡化及現代生活型態改變而持續上升。相關疾病涵蓋冠心病、高血壓及
高血脂等，並可能進一步導致心肌梗塞與中風等重大臨床事件。根據世界衛
生組織（WHO）統計，約有五分之四之心血管疾病死亡與心臟病發作及中
風相關，顯示其對全球公共健康之重大影響。

在臨床治療上，抗凝血藥物為心血管疾病管理中不可或缺之核心用藥之一，
主要用於預防及控制血栓形成，以降低心肌梗塞、中風及其他嚴重併發症之
風險。隨著民眾對心血管健康意識提升，以及疾病預防需求增加，抗凝血藥
物之臨床應用持續擴大，成為全球醫療體系中重要之基礎治療。 
在市場規模方面，全球抗凝血藥物市場於 2025 年約為 414.1 億美元，預計將
持續成長至 2034 年約 970.9 億美元 ， 年複合成長率約為9.93%， 顯示該市場

泰合產品介紹: 
➢ BEQIA(TAH3311 Apixaban ODF)抗血栓口溶膜: 
TAH3311(BEQIA)是全球首創的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥，以 BMS/Pfizer 開
發的 Eliquis 抗血栓錠劑為對照藥物。Eliquis 有效成分為 Apixaban，透過直
接抑制 Xa 凝血因子來治療和預防靜脈血栓 ，根據 BMS 財報 ，2025 年 Eliquis
全球銷售額達 144 億美元，其中美國及美國以外之銷售額分別達 102 億美元
和 42 億美元。Eliquis 佔 BMS 總營收約 29.8%，Eliquis 原料藥專利於 2028
年 10 月 17 日到期，劑型專利於 2031 年 8 月 24 日到期，為 505(b)(2)新劑
型新藥創造良好的切入時點，專利懸崖帶來營收的巨大挑戰，原廠已和解的幾
家學名藥廠預計最快將於 2028 年 4 月上市。Eliquis 除增加小兒用的粉劑外 ，
未開發出新劑型，而研發新一代的 FXIa 抗血栓藥數據臨床三期試驗療效及安
全性數據仍待觀察 ，並且抗凝血錠劑在具有吞嚥困難患者 使用痛點仍無法解
決。 
圖四：2Q26 Eliquis 營收及 2026 年財測

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

表一：2021 年後原廠 BMS/Pfizer 與 Apixaban 學名藥廠 P4 訴訟和解狀況

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

TAH3311(BEQIA)為泰合重點開發產品，其主要成分為 Apixaban，並採用口
溶膜（orodispersible film, ODF）劑型設計。Apixaban 透過選擇性抑制凝
血因子 Xa，可有效降低中風與全身性血栓風險，而降低非瓣膜性心房顫動
(NVAF)成年患者發生中風和全身性栓塞的風險是最大的應用，佔 Eliquis 總
銷售額的 70%。BEQIA 口溶膜鎖定改善高齡患者吞嚥困難的用藥痛點，相較
於 Eliquis 是一天吃兩錠需要搭配喝水，BEQIA 是一天吃兩片，僅需放入口
中即可快速溶解完成用藥，不須喝水、沒有噎嗆風險，優化長照與醫療機構
給藥流程。2025 年美國有 1,055 萬名心房顫動(AF)患者，其中約 75%之
791 萬名患者為 65 歲以上高齡患者，這當中 50~75%有吞嚥困難，故
BEQIA 在美國目標病患族群達 395.5 萬~593 萬人，隱含目標市場規模
110~164 億美元。

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

TAH3311(BEQIA)於 2026 年 6 月 22 日重新送件美國 FDA，進入 10 個月
PDUFA 審查期 ，2027 年 4 月 22 日為審核完成目標日期 ， 可望於2Q27 取得
美國暫時性核准(Tentative Approval) 。歐洲 EMA 方面，則於 2026 年 7 月
17 日完成 Day 121 關鍵清單回覆，朝 CHMP 最終審查意見(Day 210) 與 EC
核准推進，公司預計4Q26 可望獲歐盟上市許可。授權策略方面，泰合採取原
廠與非原廠雙軌並行。對原廠而言，BEQIA 可借力其成熟通路快速推升口溶
模市占，並在面臨專利懸崖與學名藥競爭時，可藉此維持利潤表現，洽談時即
以解決原廠現有困境的定位切入。至於非原廠，泰合採精準區域分擊，鎖定主
要市場以外的區域優先展開談判 ， 累積市場實績與聲量 。 泰合目前已在歐美 、
紐澳、拉丁美洲等地區與數十家藥廠簽訂 NDA，並持續洽談，多家跨國藥廠
與各區域 BD 代表已進入實質洽談階段。

圖八：BEQIA 授權模式: 原廠與非原廠雙軌並行

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

➢ TAH3341 長效劑型: 
在抗凝血治療市場中 ， 患者多為高齡且需長期甚至終生用藥族群 ， 臨床上普遍
存在多重用藥及吞嚥困難等問題，進而影響用藥便利性與治療依從性。儘管
Apixaban（Eliquis®）已為目前凝血因子 Xa 抑制劑中之領導產品， 
具備良好療效與安全性 ， 其每日兩次給藥之頻率及傳統錠劑劑型 ， 對部分患者
而言仍存在使用負擔 ， 顯示在既有標準治療基礎上 ， 透過劑型與給藥方式創新
以提升依從性，仍具明確之未被滿足醫療需求。 
TAH3341 為泰合延伸既有 Apixaban 產品線所開發之長效抗血栓口溶膜產
品，結合口溶膜（orodispersible film, ODF ）與先進緩釋技術，將原需每日
兩次服用之錠劑優化為 「每日一次」 之創新劑型。產品可於口腔中迅速崩解並
釋放藥物，使患者無需吞嚥或飲水即可完成給藥；同時，透過精密設計之控釋
機制 ， 使有效成分於體內持續穩定釋放 ， 維持適當血中濃度並達成長時間抗凝
效果。 此一 「快速崩解× 延長釋放」 之雙重設計，有效兼顧給藥便利性與療效
穩定性，並有助於降低血中濃度波動所帶來之潛在風險。 
在產品定位上，TAH3341 於維持 Apixaban 既有療效與安全性基礎上，進一
步透過降低給藥頻次與改善劑型使用方式 ， 顯著提升患者用藥依從性與長期治
療持續性。特別針對高齡族群、吞嚥困難患者及多重用藥族群，此一劑型可有
效降低給藥障礙並優化治療體驗。同時，產品亦有助於簡化用藥流程，降低照
護者負擔並提升整體用藥管理效率。TAH3341 透過 「標準治療藥物× 長效化
設計× 創新劑型平台」之整合策略，不僅回應現有抗凝血治療在依從性與便
利性方面之未被滿足需求 ， 亦進一步強化產品差異化與臨床價值 ， 有望於持續
成長之抗血栓市場中建立具競爭力之產品定位。

### Page 1

#businessconditions #confidence #aud #australia
Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September
By Luka Belobrajdic
Economist, Westpac
14:15 October 13 2026 Share
Confidence posts its sharpest fall since the start of the Middle East conflict as elevated fuel
prices and higher interest rates weigh on business sentiment, while conditions hold steady
at weak levels. Conditions unchanged at -1 / Confidence -4pts to -13.

### Page 2

The September NAB Business Survey was in the field from 23 to 30 September, capturing ongoing
global uncertainty, still-rising fuel costs and, at least in part, the RBA’s September rate hike. Business
confidence continued to deteriorate, recording its sharpest fall since March and the onset of the Middle
East conflict. The index declined 4pts to -13 and, while remaining above the lows recorded earlier this
year, is now at its weakest level since May. At 18pts below its long-run average, the result underscores

the deep pessimism that continues to weigh on businesses. This broadly mirrors the pessimism evident
in the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer Sentiment Index, where elevated fuel prices and higher
interest rates have coalesced in a similarly weak assessment of the outlook.
The headline business conditions index held steady in negative territory at -1, albeit rising 0.25pts on
an unrounded basis. While encouraging that conditions have not deteriorated further, the index has

now remained at -1 (rounded) for two consecutive months, its weakest level since the height of the
COVID-19 pandemic in 2020. At 7pts below its long-run average, the result points to continued
pressure across the business sector.
Across the major sub-components, employment moved into negative territory, falling 3pts to -1 and
sitting 4pts below its long-run average. This marks the first outright negative reading since November
2020 and provides further evidence of the gradual easing in labour market conditions. Against the

backdrop of compressed margins, the result may also indicate firms are becoming increasingly
cautious around hiring decisions. By contrast, trading conditions rose 1pt to 4, although they remain
8pts below their long-run average. The relative resilience in trading conditions is broadly consistent with

### Page 3

Westpac DataX Card-Tracker data, which suggests nominal spending remained resilient, albeit
slowing, through the September quarter despite ongoing headwinds facing consumers. Profitability
recorded the strongest improvement across the major components, rising 3pts to -5, although the sub-
index remains deeply subdued at 10pts below its long-run average.
Capacity utilisation edged 0.2ppt lower to 82.3%. While the monthly decline provides a tentative signal

of easing capacity pressures, utilisation remains 0.9ppt above its long-run average and continues to
trend higher on a three-month average basis. Capital expenditure continued to recover from its mid-
year weakness, rising 3pts to 7. While still below the highs recorded in the first quarter, the sub-index is
now trending higher on a three-month average basis, pointing to some stabilisation in investment
intentions.
Cost growth was comparatively subdued in September, although purchase cost pressures remain

elevated. Purchase cost growth edged 0.1ppt higher to 2.4%qtr, sitting 1.1ppt above its long-run
average and continuing to weigh on margins. By contrast, labour cost growth eased 0.1ppt to 1.7%qtr,
although it remains 0.5ppt above its long-run average. Final product price growth was unchanged at
0.8%qtr, 0.2ppt above its long-run average. The widening gap between purchase cost and final product
price growth suggests firms continue to absorb a meaningful share of higher input costs, consistent

with profitability remaining deeply below average. Looking ahead, the October survey will provide an
early indication of how the card surcharging ban is affecting business costs and the extent to which
these are passed through to final prices.

### Page 4

Related articles
Among other indicators, forward orders declined 1pt to -1, pointing to continued caution around the
demand outlook. Inventories, by contrast, rose 4pts (rounded) to 7, some 5pts above their long-run
average. In combination with weak forward orders, the inventory build points to a degree of slack
emerging across the business sector.

Document 268: Compelling risk-reward in US 10-year bonds

Source PDF: all report pdf/Compelling risk-reward in US 10-year bonds.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

Compelling risk-reward in 
US 10-year bonds  
Summary 
Event: The US 10 -year government bond yield has surged 
past 5.0% to a 19-year high and has now entered our three -
month forecast range of 5.0-5.25%. At the time of writing, the 
10-year yield is trading at 5.2%, its highest since June 2007’s 
high of 5.32%. The next resistance levels are the 2002 high 
of 5.47% and the 2001 high of 5.53%.  
We view the risk/reward for the US 10-year government 
bond after the recent yield spike as  compelling and we 
are initiating a long opportunistic idea at the current 
yield. As the table on the right shows, if the 10 -year yield 
rises to 6%, investors would face less than 1% in negative 
total returns over a one-year period, whereas if the yield falls 
below 5.0%, they would gain at least 6% in total returns.  
While we continue to prefer the 3-7-year bond maturity range 
for income generation (the US 5-year bond yield is also 
above 5%), and broadly prefer corporate over government 
bonds amid solid corporate fundamentals, the US 10 -year 
government bond yield has finally reached reasonable levels 
for investors looking for slightly longer maturities. 
Rationale: We expect the US 10-year government bond yield 
to ease to 4.75-5.0% over a 12-month period due to: 
Easing oil prices and inflation over next 12 months: While 
the yield is likely to remain elevated in the next three months 
due to elevated oil prices caused by geopolitical uncertainty, 
heavy AI investment-driven bond issuance and a resilient US 
economy, it is likely to ease towards the 4.75 -5.0% range 
over 12  months as oil prices moderate either due to a 
resolution in the Middle East or a rerouting of Middle East 
energy supplies and as the impact of tariffs fade.  
We expect s hallower Fed rate hikes than market s: We 
expect a total of 50bps of Fed rate hikes by June 2027, before 
a cut in H2 as US inflation eventually eases towards the Fed’s 
2% target. This  rate trajectory is shallower than c. 95bps of 
further hikes priced by markets over the next 12 months. 
 The benchmark US 10-year government bond yield has 
risen to its highest since 2007, offering medium-term 
investors a compelling risk-reward trade-off

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 28 September 2026

Investor positioning adjustment: In the very near-term, the 
US 10 -year bond yield has room to move lower as hedge 
funds using systematic strategies (CTAs) are extremely short 
US government bonds, raising the chance of short -covering 
if upcoming jobs and inflation data miss estimates. 
A shallow Fed hiking cycle  
We expect the shallowest Fed hiking cycle in almost 
three decades. This should enable the US 10-year yield to 
ease towards 4.75 -5.0% over a 12 -month period. Following 
this month’s increase, we forecast two further 25bp hikes by 
June 2027, followed by a cut in H2 2027. Markets price at 
least three more hikes by June and a strong probability of a 
fourth by September. Our projected 75bp s of total hikes this 
cycle compares with 525bps of hikes in 2022–23, 225bps in 
2015–18, 425bps in 2004–06 and 175bp in 1999–2000. The 
last smaller mid-cycle move was a single 25bps hike in 1997. 
Why we expect a more cautious Fed 
The latest hike restored the Fed’s credibility after renewed 
oil-price pressures, elevated inflation and a balanced labour 
market prompted a unanimous policy vote. However, the 
following factors argue against aggressive tightening: 
Supply-driven inflation: Energy costs and AI-infrastructure 
constraints, rather than excess demand, are driving prices. 
Higher rates cannot repair supply and may weaken growth. 
Labour is no longer the main inflation source:  Labour 
supply and demand are better balanced, wage growth has 
moderated and unemployment is near full employment. AI 
further weakens the traditional Phillips Curve relationship 
between unemployment and inflation. 
Disinflation should resume:  Energy pressures may fade 
after the US mid-term elections, while the one-off inflationary 
effects of tariffs should diminish after 2025–26. 
AI raises the neutral rate , but dampens inflation:  
Investment and productivity may lift the neutral rate ( r*), the 
rate consistent with stable inflation and full employment, 
while efficiency gains limit wage-price spirals. 
Markets are already tightening  financial conditions : 
Higher bond yields have raised borrowing costs and 
restrained activity, doing part of the Fed’s work. 
The cycle began from a higher base:  The Fed funds rate 
started at 3.75%, versus 0.25% in the previous two cycles 
and 1% before that, reducing additional tightening required. 
Monetary transmission has weakened:  Wealthier 
households drive more spending but are less rate -sensitive, 
while lower -income consumers are already constrained. 
Further hikes may , therefore, curb demand less effectively 
while causing disproportionate harm to low-income families. 
 We expect a shallower Fed rate hiking cycle compared 
with market expectations

Small business conditions are deteriorating:  Higher 
financing and energy costs are weakening sentiment, as 
reflected in the latest Beige Book . The renewed 
underperformance of US small capitalisation (S&P 600) and 
mid capitalisation (S&P 400) equity indices  versus the large 
cap S&P 500 index over the past couple of months also show 
rising pressure on small businesses. 
Fiscal and financial -stability risks are rising:  Aggressive 
Fed tightening would increase US debt-servicing costs as the 
US Treasury seeks cheaper funding through shorter-maturity 
issuance, while potentially amplifying financial-system stress. 
Upside risk to bond yields 
Our view of lower bond yields carries the following risks: 
Persistent inflation:  Repeated supply shocks, strong AI -
related investment demand and tighter immigration controls 
could keep inflation above target and force steeper Fed rate 
hikes. With unemployment near the Fed’s estimate of full 
employment, a sharp drop in labour supply could intensify 
wage pressures and destabilise inflation expectations . We 
note that long-term expectations remain contained for now. 
Geopolitical escalation:  An intensification of geopolitical 
conflicts after the mid -term elections could lift energy prices 
and inflation. However, a Democrat -controlled or divided 
Congress may constrain US foreign-policy initiatives through 
tighter budgets, while Republicans could turn less supportive 
as attention shifts to the 2028 presidential race. 
Higher neutral rate: AI investments could raise the neutral 
rate (r*), turning current monetary policy settings 
accommodative. This could necessitate further rate hikes. 
However, the Fed has barely raised its nominal neutral rate 
estimate, lifting it to 3.2% in September from 3.0% a year ago. 
Bottomline: The outlook for rates remains geopolitics 
dependent, with all Fed meetings live . However, based on 
our core scenario, we see the US 10-year yield settling lower 
in the 4.75-5.0% in 6-12 months. A shallow Fed hiking cycle 
due to expected disinflation offers investors a reasonably 
attractive risk-reward in US 10-year government bonds. 
US 30-year government bonds , on the other hand, may 
continue to see elevated yields as the US fiscal deficit is 
unlikely to have improved next year, and may look much 
worse. As a result, “term premium” may keep the ultra -long 
end elevated. Short of some unorthodox policy to artifici ally 
cap them lower, the 30-year yield is likely to continue to drift 
higher towards the 5.5-5.75% range into H2 2027. 
— Jonathan Liang, CIO for Fixed Income and FX   
Rajat Bhattacharya, Senior Investment Strategist 
Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

Document 292: Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Entering the age of the older consumer As America ages, older consumers have more leisure time, significant wealth and growing economic influence. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

13023135     1

Economy 
Entering the age of the older consumer 
01 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• The older consumer is moving center stage. America's population has been aging for decades, but the shift has accelerated: the 
share aged over 60 increased by almost 10 percentage points (pp) between 1995 and 2025. And the Census Bureau projects the 
share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population. 
• More free time means a different spending mix. People aged 65 and over spend around two and a half fewer hours per day 
working and roughly two hours more on leisure than the overall adult population. Bank of America data shows that older 
households allocate relatively more of their card spending to groceries and travel, and less to restaurants, gasoline, general 
merchandise and clothing. 
• Households aged over 55 held close to $140 trillion in net worth in the second quarter of 2026, giving many older consumers 
considerable capacity to spend on travel and other leisure categories. But wealth is not evenly distributed, and healthcare and 
other cost pressures remain significant for older households on tighter or fixed incomes.

Population aging is nothing new, but it is accelerating  
The US population has been growing relatively “older” for a long time. Exhibit 1 shows that the proportion of the population 
under 20 years old shrank from the mid-1960s through 2025. At the same time, the proportion of the population in older age 
groups has been rising.  
Exhibit 1: Over time the US population has steadily aged  
Proportion of US population by age range (%)

Source: Human Mortality Database; Bank of America Institute calculations

### Page 2

  I NSTI TUTE

Additionally, there has been a notable acceleration in the relative aging of the population in recent years. For example, between 
1995 and 2025, the proportion of the population aged over 60 rose by almost 10 percentage points (pp) to 25%.  
Several factors have driven this. First, fertility rates in the US, as in much of the world, have fallen sharply. This has slowed 
natural population growth and, by reducing the number of younger people relative to older cohorts, has increased the share of 
the population at older ages. Second, more recently, net inward migration to the US has slowed. Because migrants tend to be 
younger than the overall population, weaker immigration also tends to raise the average age. Finally, rising life expectancy over 
much of the post-war period has allowed more people to survive into older age, further increasing the older population's share.  
Exhibit 2: The trend towards an older composition of the population is expected to continue  
Forecast changes in the share of population by age group relative to 2025 (pp)

Source: United States Census Bureau 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Lower fertility and, more recently, weaker net migration look set to slow overall US population growth in the decades ahead. As a 
result, older Americans are likely to play an increasingly important role in the economy, both as a share of the population and, in 
many age groups, in absolute numbers. The Census Bureau projects the share of the population over 60 will rise another 5pp by 
2055, to stand at nearly 30% of the population (Exhibit 2). For businesses, understanding how the needs and spending patterns 
of older consumers differ from those of younger generations will therefore become increasingly relevant. 
Older age groups allocate their time differently  
Why does this matter? One reason is that older people tend to spend their time and money quite differently from younger adults. 
Most obviously, many work less or have retired altogether. Data from the American Time Use Survey (ATUS) shows that people 
aged 65 and over spend, on average, around two and a half fewer hours per day working than the population aged 15+ (Exhibit 
3). 
That leaves considerably more time for other activities. People aged 65 and over spend around two hours more per day on 
leisure and sports activities, with television viewing accounting for a particularly large share of this additional leisure time 
(Exhibit 4).

Older households also spend differently  
Bank of America internal data on credit and debit card spending provides insights into how these differences in older 
generations’ use of time also impact their spending. Rather than splitting our spending data by generation, as we often do, here 
we split by age bands, allowing for consistent comparisons over time.  
Exhibit 5 shows how the composition of card spending of households aged 61-75 and those older than 75 differs from that of all 
households. A few things stand out. For one, older households devote a greater share of their overall card spending to groceries 
(food and beverages). Households aged 61-75 also allocate more spending to travel (airlines and lodging). And restaurants and 
bars, gasoline, general merchandise and clothing account for a smaller share of older households’ card spending.  
These differences may seem fairly intuitive. Older households have more leisure hours, so spend relatively more on travel to 
enjoy them. Previously we found that more available time may support travel particularly around retirement, although the 
discretionary spending lift can be temporary (for more, read our publication: Paychecks to pensions: The evolution of retiree 
spending).  
Older households are also likely to have less need to update their work wardrobes, so they probably spend less on clothing.

Still, older households’ relatively lower spending on restaurants and bars is a little more curious, perhaps, given dining out could 
be one way of making use of more leisure time. Indeed, when we look at how these spending shares are evolving, we find a 
nuanced picture. While households aged 61-75 do spend relatively less on restaurants and bars than the overall population, they 
appear to be narrowing this gap. But for those older than 75 this is not the case. In our view, the pandemic may still be weighing 
on dining-out spending among this older age group (Exhibit 6).

Aging presents opportunities, but also challenges 
In our view, the aging of the US population presents important opportunities for the economy and businesses. Older consumers 
have more leisure time and businesses that cater to these demands may therefore benefit. 
Moreover, some older consumers hold considerable amounts of net wealth and have been some of the biggest beneficiaries of 
recent rises in equities. Using data from the Federal Reserve, Exhibit 8 shows that as of the second quarter of 2026 the net 
worth of those households aged 55 and over was close to $140 trillion, around three-quarters of the total. And with equity 
markets rising strongly, these age groups have seen a more than 20% increase in their net worth over the past two years, 
potentially enhancing their spending power further.  
But an important caveat is that not all older consumers are in this position. Previous Institute work found evidence consistent 
with equity-market wealth effects being concentrated toward higher-income households (read our publication: Paychecks to 
pensions: The evolution of retiree spending). And Census Bureau research has found that around 14% of Social Security 
recipients aged over 65 rely on it for over 90% of their income. For older households without a wealth cushion, recent elevated 
cost pressures, like gasoline, will likely have put them under more significant financial strain. They will also be more dependent 
on cost-of-living (COLA) increases in Social Security to underpin their spending.  
And, more broadly, outside of card spending, healthcare costs are a much larger factor in older households’ budgets (Exhibit 9). 
The evolution of these costs going forward will be a key variable for older consumers’ budgets.
